# Epic encounters: what the scripts do, and what bots would have to do

Generated by `source/tools/epic_encounter_catalog.py` from the boss scripts in `source/server/GameServer/scripts/namedmobs/` and the hand checks in `docs/encounters/hand-notes.json`. Don't edit this file: edit the hand notes and run the generator (`--check` tells CI whether it is up to date). The machine-readable version is [epic-encounters.json](epic-encounters.json).

**This is a catalog, step 1 of "bots play epic encounters properly". Nothing in it is implemented.** Every tactic is a proposal. Darkness Falls raid AI, Legion and the hardest level 70+ encounters have no raid AI; they are listed, marked *no raid AI*. No rule here was checked in game: "verified" means a person read the rule against its script.

- Scripts read: 214. Encounters: 238 (228 with rules, 2 written by hand).
- Rules: 862; verified against the script: 862; confidence read directly: 493, inferred: 369.
- By kind: adds 155, aggro_wipe 11, damage_reduced 3, empowered 16, hazard 3, heal 26, immune_while 66, linked_pack 119, offline_change 23, pet_devour 2, phase 57, position_puzzle 10, reflect 8, reset 277, teleport 32, tether 54.

Difficulty: *easy for the current raid AI* (it would do this with small changes, or already does), *needs new raid AI* (target choice, splitting, timing the raid doesn't have), *unsafe for bots today* (bots can't recover from it yet), *off today* (an `EpicEncounterMechanics` switch disables it, or the script only ever picks real players: `GameBot` is a `GameNPC`, not a `GamePlayer`, so `GetPlayersInRadius` and `is GamePlayer` never choose a bot).

## Encounters with raid tactics

The raid AI (`EpicEncounterTactics`, autonomous gamebots only) plays these tactics, read from the hand notes' `aiTactics` into `EpicEncounterTactics.Catalog.cs`. The ones new in 1.2 are tested by unit and harness tests only and have not been seen in a run yet (log lines: `REALM_EXPEDITION_TACTIC`, `EPIC_TACTIC_BOT`).

| Encounter | Since | Tactic |
|---|---|---|
| Dra'argus the Mighty (Avalon City) | **1.2 (new)** | Required add, from range (1.2): while the Drau'gyn Sphere burns he takes no damage and doesn't attack, and the sphere (10,000 health, floating above the floor) burns everyone within 500 for 300 every 4 s and pulls raiders at its edge into its middle. Casters and archers kill it from at least 650; melee, tanks and healers wait 850 from it, then everyone fights him (EpicEncounterTactics.AvalonCity). |
| Sys'sro the Ruthless (Avalon City) | **1.2 (new)** | Hazards (1.2): his 15 pit monsters can't be hurt (and die with him), so nobody attacks them; a raider within 400 of one steps out. His throw (every 10-15 s, a raider other than his target into the pit 1,200 away) takes bots too: a thrown bot walks out of the pit back to its party, ignoring the pit monsters that snare it, or is recalled if it can't. |
| Fames (Caer Sidi) | **1.2 (new)** | Fight at home (1.2, with the original tether heal on): past 2,700 from his spawn (his tether is 3,600, where he heals to full) the raid stops hitting him and the raider he chases leads him back. |
| Bellum (Caer Sidi) | **1.2 (new)** | Fight at home (1.2, with the original tether heal on): past 2,700 from his spawn (his tether is 3,600, where he heals to full) the raid stops hitting him and the raider he chases leads him back. |
| Morbus (Caer Sidi) | **1.2 (new)** | Required adds (1.2, with his original swarm shield on): while a swarm of morbus lives (6-10 a wave, 15,000 health, weak to cold, every 25-40 s once the last wave is dead) he takes no damage and stops fighting, so the raid kills the swarm (each party takes one) and turns back on him once it is gone. Fight at home (1.2, with the original tether heal on): past 2,700 from his spawn (his tether is 3,600, where he heals to full) the raid stops hitting him and the raider he chases leads him back. |
| Funus (Caer Sidi) | **1.2 (new)** | Class and weapon puzzle (1.2, with the original on): any hit from the wrong class or weapon (pets too) heals him to full. Wave 3: clerics fight him as they like; scouts, mercenaries, armsmen and infiltrators who own their listed weapon (longbow, short bow, crossbow: gamebots start with a training one of their class's own kind when they learn the line and earn better ones) draw it and fight with it only: BotBrain never falls back to melee on him (out of endurance they wait), the native close-range melee switch is skipped, and unless they hold him they step back to 700 when he is within 450. Everyone else drops him, calls its pet off and waits 1,400 away (kept inside the room while its door is barred), and never puts an area spell where he stands. No bot's pet attacks him, takes him from an order or defends its owner against him (EpicEncounterTactics.PetMustNotHit). A damage shield's sting from a bot he can't be hurt by neither heals nor hurts him (server property epic_funus_bot_shield_guard, on; real players keep the original). Fight at home (1.2, with the original tether heal on): past 2,700 from his spawn (his tether is 3,600, where he heals to full) the raid stops hitting him and the raider he chases leads him back. Not played: how many of a raid's mercenaries, armsmen and infiltrators own the weapon depends on their earned gear. |
| Crypt Lord (Caer Sidi) | **1.2 (new)** | His court comes with him (1.2): he never walks home, once he fights his whole court within 10,000 (43 with PackageID CryptLordBaf) joins on his target, and a court monster fighting within 800 of him pulls him in. So the route never takes a court monster as a small pull of its own while he lives: it fights him instead, a mustered encounter (half the raid gathers first), and the court comes with him (EpicEncounterTactics.CaerSidi route rule). |
| Skeletal Sacristan (Caer Sidi) | **1.2 (new)** | Ambush stations (1.2, with his original patrol on: 220 without stopping, 70% magic resists; he never attacks): each party waits at one of 11 stations along his loop (its seven corners and the middle of every leg over 2,400) and hits him while he passes (melee within 450 of the station, casters, archers and healers within 1,400), then waits for his next round (EpicEncounterTactics.CaerSidi). |
| Soul Reckoner (Caer Sidi) | **1.2 (new)** | Required adds (1.2, with his original full shield on): while a reckoned soul lives (4-6 at the start, 1-2 more every 60-70 s) he takes no damage, so the raid kills the souls first, each party one. His room (1.2, the original 900 from its middle): past 700 from the room's middle the raid stops hitting him, keeping him as its target, and the raider he chases leads him back in (EpicEncounterTactics.CaerSidi). |
| Xanxicar (Crystal Cave) | **1.2 (new)** | Blasts: within 3% above 80/60/40/20% and for 9 s after crossing one, everyone except the party he is fighting withdraws 2,700 from him; that party pushes him over and takes the 2,500-radius stomp (it is meant to kill whoever is in it), the rest of the raid survives it and comes back. 1.2 wave 2: when he bellows, that party's healers top it up first; when he prepares his glare (450 around a raider, 5 s), everyone near that raider steps away and healers heal him (EncounterSignals). |
| Cuuldurach the Glimmer King (Cuuldurach's lair (Sheeroe Hills)) | **1.2 (new)** | Messengers (on since 1.2, AllowDragonMessengers): from 50% every 80-90 s four to six messengers (CuuldurachMessenger, 1,500 health, they never fight) run out of the lair and each calls four to six adds where its path ends; the raid's casters and archers run each one down (ranged adds) while melee and healers stay on the dragon. Stare (glare, 1,000 around the raider it picks, 6 s warning): everyone within it but the picked raider and the tank steps out, away from him, and healers heal him first. Look-around stun and breath (2,000 around the dragon, 6 s warning): casters, archers and healers step out past 2,250 until it lands; melee can't make it and take it. Each one is called in /group (EncounterSignals, EncounterCalls). |
| Legion (Darkness Falls) | **1.2 (new)** | Tactic rules only (Darkness Falls raid AI as a whole is not implemented: no platform positions, no walked way down from the platform, no add-wave assignments). Graspering souls (one per four raiders within 2,000 at each 5% step, at most 16 a step): casters and archers kill them while melee stay on Legion. Close range: he takes no damage from attackers more than 1,400 from him, so a raider farther out steps in to 1,100. Kill heals (2.5% per raider he kills): healers near him heal anyone under half first, and since wave 3 a raider under 35% who is not his target steps out to 1,700 until healed. Bubbles (wave 3): he ends the bladeturn on his target every turn and after five casts his soul blast (1,000 body damage within 1,000 of his target, a quarter healing him), so no bot casts a bladeturn (buff, group bubble, pulsing chant, self bubble) within 2,500 of him while he fights. His throw (wave 3): 8-16 raiders below his top five on his aggro list go to his platform every 40-65 s, bots too; a bot has no walked way down, so its recovery recalls it to its party after 5 s. |
| Prince Asmoien (Darkness Falls) | **1.2 (new)** | Pets out (1.2): he eats the nearest summoned pet within 2,000 every 5 s and heals by its health (real pets only, never a bot), so a raider with a pet drops him, calls its pet off and waits 2,400 away with it; everyone else fights him (EpicEncounterTactics.DarknessFalls). |
| Prince Ba'alorien (Darkness Falls) | **1.2 (new)** | Pets out (1.2): he eats the nearest summoned pet within 2,000 every 5 s and heals by its health (real pets only, never a bot), so a raider with a pet drops him, calls its pet off and waits 2,400 away with it; everyone else fights him (EpicEncounterTactics.DarknessFalls). |
| Krackenschtein (Fomor City) | **1.2 (new)** | Close in: he takes damage only from within 300, so every raider, casters too, fights from within 220-280 of him. His throw (four spots 465 above his floor) takes bots too since 1.2; they fall and walk back. |
| Myrddraxis (Fomor City) | **1.2 (new)** | Breaths at health thresholds: within 3% above 80/60/40/20% (stunning breath, 30 s stun in 1,800) and 75/50/25% (Breath of Annihilation, 2,000 heat in 1,800), and for 9 s after crossing one, everyone but the party he fights stands 2,350 from him; the 5-6 s warning in the script lands inside that window. Fomor City has no raid of its own (no path in on its navmesh), so this steers the gamebots that fight there. |
| Aroon the Urlamhai (Galladoria) | **1.2 (new)** | Damage order (AllowAroonDamageOrder, on since 1.2): each Scaith is immune until the one before it is dead (slash, thrust, crush, body, cold, energy, heat, matter, spirit), and Aroon takes only the damage types whose Scaith has died. The whole raid kills the Scaiths one at a time in that order (a Scaith that is not next is held by the party it fights), then Aroon. |
| Conservator (Galladoria) | **1.2 (new)** | Close range (AllowPositionPuzzle, on since 1.2): he takes damage only from attackers within 800 of his spawn. A raider farther out steps in to 500; fight him at home: drawn past 600 from his spawn the raid stops hitting him and whoever he chases leads him back. |
| Easmarach (Galladoria) | **1.2 (new)** | Close range (AllowPositionPuzzle, on since 1.2): he takes damage only from attackers within his 2,500 tether of his spawn; a raider farther out steps in. The waterfall top (wave 3, 1.2): below 30% he clears his aggro, walks up to 37,811,50,342 (about 1,100 above his spawn, no walked path) and heals an eighth of his health; casters, archers and healers go to their party's spot on the floor below (six spots the raid reached on 2026-11-06, each within 1,250 of the top and 2,500 of his spawn) and shoot him from there, melee wait at his spawn (EpicEncounterTactics.Galladoria). He heals there once a fight, rests 50-80 s and walks back down to his spawn (1.2 play test 2026-11-07: up there for good, healing at every fall to 30%, a bot raid wore him from 42% to 34% in ten minutes and could never finish him); the original endless heal is the owner setting epic_easmarach_endless_falls_heal (off). His push behind the falls (raiders within 1,000 of 37,653,52,843 are moved to 40,067,50,494) is on since wave 3, for bots too: while he lives the route never sends the raid to the last room (the bridge, the primals, Olcasgean), it fights him first; a bot pushed back stays where the push put it and a repeated push is logged (EPIC_DISPLACEMENT_BOUNCED). Container play test 2026-11-07: the close range and the falls stands played as written (all six stands on the navmesh, walked both ways); sight to the top is not checked by the mesh. |
| Olcasgean (Galladoria) | **1.2 (new)** | Safe spots (AllowPositionPuzzle, on since 1.2): unless a raider stands within 200 of 40,170,62,600 (his copy) and one within 200 of 39,237,62,644 (him) he casts his Root DD without pause; the raid's two lowest-numbered parties hold one spot each the whole fight and fight the body that stands there (they share health), everyone else fights where it likes. Below half he throws one raider every 12-20 s who was not thrown before (bots too since 1.2): it walks back (EncounterDisplacementRecovery). The Waterfall Antipass pushes anyone who tries to leave back into the room until he dies. The party calls an empty spot in /group. |

Close in: he takes damage only from within 200 of him and the shards only from within 400 of his spot, so every raider, casters too, fights from within 120-180 of him or 320 of his spot. |
| Icelord Kvasir (Tuscaran Glacier) | **1.2 (new)** | Cold heal (AllowKvasirColdHeal, on since 1.2): no bot casts a cold spell at him (EpicEncounterTactics.ForbidsSpell; casters use their other lines). Scream: from 3 s before his primal scream is due until 4.5 s after his mesmerize (800 around his target, 2 s after the scream), everyone but his target's party stands 1,050 from his target; melee wait there, casters and healers keep casting. |
| Jailer Vifil (Tuscaran Glacier) | **1.2 (new)** | Jail rescue (AllowDisplacement, bots too since 1.2): the jail opens when his two tunnel guardians (20,000 health each, at the jail) are dead. Once the fight is on, one bot party (two in a raid of eight parties or more; never the party he fights or the real player's) goes down the tunnel, kills both and comes back (an errand: no path, no progress or past its budget and it rejoins the fight). Every raider jailed after that walks free at once; one jailed before fights the guards with the team, and the recovery frees it when they die (or recalls it after 3 minutes). |
| Queen Kula (Tuscaran Glacier) | **1.2 (new)** | Killed first (see King Tuscar). Her drag to the throne (AllowDisplacement, bots too since 1.2) puts a raider in her frost circle, where it is mesmerized or rooted: a bot waits it out like a player (the recovery's walk does not count crowd control as a stall; at most 5 minutes) and walks back. Frost circle (AllowPositionPuzzle, on): no raider fights within 450 of 34128,56095; one that gets there steps out to 600. |
| King Tuscar (Tuscaran Glacier) | **1.2 (new)** | Twin heal (AllowTuscarTwinHeal, on since 1.2): damage to one twin heals the other while it is hurt, so the raid kills Queen Kula first (the route musters for her) and only the King's own target fights him; the rest of his party turns on her too. Casters and healers on Kula keep clear of his hammers and Thor's Might (500 around his target). Nobody stands in Kula's frost circle. |
| Aidon the Archwizard (Hall of the Corrupt) | 1.1.2 | Required adds, keep one: once the fight is on the raid kills three of his four illusions (each party takes one) and leaves the last alive, because he summons a new set only when all four are dead; then everyone fights him. Fight at home: past 1,100 from his spawn (tether 1,500) the raid stops hitting him and leads him back. |
| Scurceol Hyrde (Krondon) | 1.1.2 | Required adds: the four Lyft Miht orbs are the raid's targets while any lives (each party takes one; fought from a floor spot within 1,400 that the raid can reach and that sees the orb; melee that can't reach a flying orb waits at that spot); nobody hits him until the last orb is dead. Fight at home: past 1,500 from his spawn the raid stops hitting him and the raider he chases leads him back. |
| Ancient Black Oak (Marfach Caverns) | 1.1.2 | Dies with Black Lady: while she lives the raid fights her instead of the Oak. |
| Nosdoden (Trollheim) | 1.1.2 | Waves as required adds: when a wave of essences spawns (every 10% from 90%), the raid stops hitting him and kills the wave, then goes back to him; so his health falls one step at a time and waves don't stack. |

## Restored behind switches

Where the fork had changed a mechanic for bots with no switch, the original is back behind an `EpicEncounterMechanics` switch. Each was off (the game played as before) until the raid AI could play it; the ones it plays are on since 1.2. Each restore switch is its own server property (`/serverproperty`), read live, so it can be changed without a rebuild. Originals read from OpenDAoC/OpenDAoC-Core 3513bad49912 (2026-11-04) and OpenDAoC/OpenDAoC-Database 7068aef09830, cloned 2026-11-05. Upstream has been refactored since this fork's base (instance fields instead of statics, ProcessDeath), so the behaviour was read, not copied.

| Encounter | Places | Original | Fork today | Switch | Default (setting) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| caer-sidi/fames, caer-sidi/morbus, caer-sidi/funus | 3 | Out of its tether a horseman heals to full and drops its aggro. | It drops its aggro and keeps its health (heals only when the fight is over). | `AllowHorsemenTetherHeal` | on (`npc / epic_horsemen_tether_heal`) |
| caer-sidi/funus | 1 | Only clerics, and mercenaries with a short bow, armsmen and infiltrators with a crossbow, scouts with a longbow damage Funus; any other attacker (pets too) heals him to full. | Everyone damages him. | `AllowFunusWeaponPuzzle` | off (`npc / epic_funus_weapon_puzzle`) |
| caer-sidi/skeletalsacristan | 2 | He walks his loop without stopping (195-250 in this fork's base; upstream now a 220 path) with 70% magic resists. | He walks at 130-160, stands still while hit, 55% magic resists. | `AllowSacristanPatrol` | on (`npc / epic_sacristan_patrol`) |
| caer-sidi/soulreckoner | 2 | No damage at all while a reckoned soul lives, or outside a 900 room. | A quarter of the damage while a soul lives; the room is 1,500. | `AllowSoulReckonerFullShield, AllowSoulReckonerSmallRoom` | on (`npc / epic_soul_reckoner_full_shield`), on (`npc / epic_soul_reckoner_small_room`) |
| galladoria/aroon | 9 | The nine Scaiths die in order (slash, thrust, crush, body, cold, energy, heat, matter, spirit): each is immune until the one before is dead, and Aroon takes only the damage types whose Scaith has died (nothing at first). | Any damage type hurts Aroon and every Scaith. | `AllowAroonDamageOrder` | on (`npc / epic_aroon_damage_order`) |
| galladoria/olcasgeaninitializator | 1 | The primals come when a real player (not a bot) stands within 350 of the bridge spot. | A raid bot within 1,500 of the spot starts it (players still 350). | `AllowGalladoriaBridgeSpot (bots within 350; a bot-only rule would be unwinnable)` | on (`npc / epic_galladoria_bridge_spot`) |
| galladoria/olcasgean | 1 | Split-position and throw puzzle. | Off. | `AllowPositionPuzzle (already a switch; nothing new)` | off |
| galladoria/fireprimal | 1 | Trail of fire: 220 damage in a 450 radius. | 120 in 300. | `AllowFullFirePatches` | off (`npc / epic_full_fire_patches`) |
| galladoria/earthprimal | 4 | Each Earthmender takes damage only from its class group (Guardian: blademaster, hero, champion; Magical: eldritch, enchanter, mentalist; Natural: bard, druid, warden; Shadowy: nightshade, ranger; animists, valewalkers and pets hurt all four); they never attack; they heal to full 30 s after a fight; they heal each other and the primal. | Since 1.2 the class rule is back on (AllowEarthmenderClassPuzzle) and the raid assigns classes to it. Still changed: they fight back; no quick reset while raiders are in the room; they heal the primal only. | `AllowEarthmenderClassPuzzle, AllowEarthmendersPassive, AllowEarthmenderQuickReset, AllowEarthmenderMutualHeal` | on (`npc / epic_earthmender_class_puzzle`), on (`npc / epic_earthmenders_passive`), on (`npc / epic_earthmender_quick_reset`), on (`npc / epic_earthmender_mutual_heal`) |
| galladoria/beatha | 1 | Beatha's damage heals Xaga by twice the amount. | No heal. | `AllowBeathaHealsXaga` | on (`npc / epic_beatha_heals_xaga`) |
| galladoria/beatha, galladoria/tine | 2 | The fairies circle the room (250, flying) even while fought. | They hold still while fought. | `AllowFairyFlight` | on (`npc / epic_fairy_flight`) |

| tuscaran-glacier/kvasir | 1 | Cold damage heals him a fifth of his health and does nothing else (upstream has no out-of-tether immunity for him). | Cold hurts him. | `AllowKvasirColdHeal` | on (`npc / epic_kvasir_cold_heal`) |
| tuscaran-glacier/queenkula, tuscaran-glacier/kingtuscar | 2 | Damage to one twin heals the other by the same amount while it is below full health. | No heal. | `AllowTuscarTwinHeal` | on (`npc / epic_tuscar_twin_heal`) |

**Scurceol's orbs upstream:** Upstream's Lyft Miht orbs are the same: level 90, 15,000 health, 40/50% resists, AF 250, flying at the same four corners. Upstream only replaced the static orb counters with a per-boss list (a bug fix: our static counters are shared by every Scurceol and can drift).

**Helper packs upstream:** OpenDAoC-Database mob.sql compared with the 0.37b release world: of the 47 packages empty in our world, 42 are empty upstream too (the script looks for helpers that never existed: DebenBaf, FamesBaf, MorbusBaf, HatiBaf, SkollBaf, ApocBaf ...). Five have upstream spawns our world lacks: AncientOakBaf (14 "Ancient Oak" in Black Lady's room, none in ours), CaimheulBaf (6 reanimated guardians; 1 in ours, under PackageID offline-classic-frontier-dungeon-restored), CailleachUragaigBaf (3 cailleach guards), RevenantBaf (11 wraiths and wights by the Raumarik Revenant), RunilBaf (4 clan deserters). Not changed here: adding world spawns is a world update for the owner to decide.

## Player-only mechanics (candidates to target bots later)

These still pick real players only, so bots are never chosen. Since 1.2 the scripts pick through `EncounterTargets` (real players and bots); what is left here are the moves (throws, teleports, jails), whose picks include bots only once `EncounterTargets.BotsCanBeDisplaced` is on, and anything still on `GetPlayersInRadius` / `is GamePlayer`.

- Scurceol Hyrde (Krondon): teleport `ScurceolHyrde.cs:684`: An orb hit by a real player has a 25% chance (once per pick) to throw that player, 8-15 s later, to one of four spots on the floor and deal 20% of their health 
- Moran the Mighty (Open world): teleport `MoranTheMighty.cs:179`: Throws portPlayer[ranPlayer] into the air or across the room: one of 3 spots (MoveTo 1, 401943, 753091, 222, 3499; 1, 406787, 749150, 213, 3926; 1, 401061, 7558

## Priority: encounters that block raids today

### 1. Scurceol Hyrde (Krondon): immune while his four orbs live

**Run evidence:** docs/NIGHT-SIEGES-AND-MARFACH.md §1b: build 8d7baa3, 2026-11-05 14:27-15:40. 59-77 of 101 raiders fought him 3,219 units from his spawn; he stayed at 100%; all 42 raider deaths were to him; he was skipped every 20 minutes and switched back to; the Beran muster stalled at 2-5 of 50. 'Lyft' is in no log line. 2026-11-06 (168f55d): the tactic fired at 01:49 and 02:16, but every orb failed the ground-route gate and was set aside; the raid went to Fuladl.

**Why it blocks:** No damage is possible until the orbs die, and the raid never targets them: the orbs never attack, so the 'fighting the raid' switch never picks them, and melee can't reach them.

**Proposed tactic:** Fight him in his own room, not 3,000 units out where the raid met him: one tank party holds him near his spawn while four ranged parties take one orb each from the floor below it (the route already shoots flyers from below), then everyone on him. Orb kills survive his resets.

Rules of Scurceol Hyrde (`source/server/GameServer/scripts/namedmobs/Krondon/ScurceolHyrde.cs`):

- **immune_while** (verified): Takes no damage of any type while any of the four Lyft Miht orbs (LyftMihtOne-Four, static counters Orb1Count-Orb4Count) is alive. `ScurceolHyrde.cs:33`. *needs new raid AI*: In the raid AI (EpicEncounterTactics, required adds): while an orb lives the raid's target is an orb, each party takes the orb its index points at, casters and archers shoot it from the floor, melee stands off under it, and nobody hits him. Then the whole raid turns on him.
- **adds** (verified): Spawns his four Lyft Miht orbs when he spawns, one per room corner, only if that orb's counter is 0; they never respawn by themselves. `ScurceolHyrde.cs:132`. *needs new raid AI*: Count orb kills as permanent progress: a wipe or reset on Scurceol does not undo them.
- **tether** (verified): Tether range 3800: drawn further from its spawn it walks home. `ScurceolHyrde.cs:97`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Fight it where it stands; don't pull it past its tether.
- **reset** (verified): Resets when its aggro list is empty: walks home at full health `ScurceolHyrde.cs:171`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Keep someone on its aggro list until it dies; a wipe resets it (the 8 s quiet rule keeps a big raid's gaps from resetting it).
- **teleport** (verified): An orb hit by a real player has a 25% chance (once per pick) to throw that player, 8-15 s later, to one of four spots on the floor and deal 20% of their health as falling damage. The same code is in all four orb brains. `ScurceolHyrde.cs:684`. *off today*: Off for bots today: it picks only real players (GameBot is a GameNPC, not a GamePlayer). To play it: a thrown bot walks back to its orb party.
- **empowered** (verified): Strength 320 instead of 260 while any orb lives (he hits harder until the orbs die). `ScurceolHyrde.cs:180`. *needs new raid AI*: Another reason to kill the orbs first; the tank party needs extra healing until then.

### 2. Black Lady and her Ancient Black Oak (Marfach Caverns)

**Run evidence:** docs/NIGHT-SIEGES-AND-MARFACH.md §1: 2026-11-05 01:28-05:19, 91 pulls on the Oak, never in combat, 0 raiders within 1,000; Black Lady leashed home from 44% after being drawn 1,618 from her spawn. Timed out. 2026-11-05 (168f55d): won with the dies-with tactic; Black Lady died in 1.2 minutes.

**Why it blocks:** The Oak never pulls itself (AggroLevel 0) and stands inside its tree; the raid fought Black Lady away from her spawn so she leashed and healed.

**Proposed tactic:** Skip the Oak: it dies when Black Lady dies. Fight Black Lady where she stands (don't drag her), one party on Ogresses. PR #77 already fights the Oak from a walkable approach and sets it aside after three empty pulls.

Rules of Ancient Black Oak (`source/server/GameServer/scripts/namedmobs/MarfachCaverns/AncientBlackOak.cs`):

- **immune_while** (verified): Takes no damage while it is drawn past its tether. `AncientBlackOak.cs:54`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Fight inside the tether: stop hitting it once it is drawn out and lead it home (AutonomousTetherReset already does this).
- **reset** (verified): Resets when its aggro list is empty: walks home at full health `AncientBlackOak.cs:161`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Keep someone on its aggro list until it dies; a wipe resets it (the 8 s quiet rule keeps a big raid's gaps from resetting it).
- **linked_pack** (verified): When pulled it puts every living NPC with PackageID "AncientOakBaf" in the region on its aggro list. The release world database has no such NPC, so today it brings nobody. `AncientBlackOak.cs:176`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Nothing to do today; if the world gets AncientOakBaf mobs, clear or pull them first.

Rules of Black Lady (`source/server/GameServer/scripts/namedmobs/MarfachCaverns/BlackLady.cs`):

- **immune_while** (verified): Takes no damage while it is drawn past its tether. `BlackLady.cs:24`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Fight inside the tether: stop hitting it once it is drawn out and lead it home (AutonomousTetherReset already does this).
- **adds** (verified): Spawns the Ancient Black Oak (a separate epic encounter, RespawnInterval -1) when she spawns; her death kills it. `BlackLady.cs:122`. *needs new raid AI*: In the raid AI (EpicEncounterTactics, dies with): the raid fights Black Lady instead of the Oak; the Oak dies with her. She is fought within 1,100 of her spawn (fight at home).
- **adds** (verified): Each brain turn in combat (every 2 s), a 20% chance to spawn one Ogress (level 50-55, 2,500 health, aggro 1,200) at one of two spots, up to 5 alive. Ogresses put healer and caster classes on their aggro list at 150 and others at 10 (real players and bots since 1.2, EncounterTargets). `BlackLady.cs:207`. *easy for the current raid AI*: One party kills Ogresses as they appear; they are script-spawned adds, so the route already puts them first.
- **reset** (verified): Resets when its aggro list is empty: walks home at full health, adds cleared `BlackLady.cs:167`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Keep someone on its aggro list until it dies; a wipe resets it (the 8 s quiet rule keeps a big raid's gaps from resetting it).

### 3. Nosdoden (Trollheim): nine waves of 12-18 adds

**Run evidence:** overnight-2026-11-04 checks 5 and day run: Defeated twice in a row at Nosdoden (support 80, then 104; and 132 at 135% size), Special Forces joined each time.

**Why it blocks:** Each 10% step spawns 12-18 level 60 adds at his feet; a big raid's damage pushes several steps at once and the waves stack.

**Proposed tactic:** Pace the damage one 10% step at a time; at each threshold stop on him and AoE the wave down; healers stay with the tank.

Rules of Nosdoden (`source/server/GameServer/scripts/namedmobs/Trollheim/Nosdoden.cs`):

- **adds** (verified): EnemyKilled: when he kills a raider (a real player, or a bot since 1.2) he makes a 'Spirit of <name>' ghost with that raider's race, size, armour and class behaviour. Flood guard (1.2): no new spirit while 64 are up (Trollheim's period raid; EncounterFloodGuard 'Nosdoden.Spirits'). `Nosdoden.cs:167`. *needs new raid AI*: A dead raider's ghost fights the raid: kill it, or res the raider (the ghost dies when its owner lives again).
- **adds** (verified): SpawnEssences: about 12-18 essence of fallen (NosdodenSummonedAdds, StandardMobBrain-based, aggro range 1,500) around him at each of nine thresholds (90, 80, ... 10%). `Nosdoden.cs:683`. *needs new raid AI*: Push him one 10% step at a time: stop damage on him at each threshold, AoE classes and two parties burn the wave down at his feet, healers keep the tank up, then the next step. Don't let three waves stack.
- **adds** (verified): Spawns spirit champion (GhostSpiritChampion): from SummonSpiritChampion; count 1 spawn call in SummonSpiritChampion `Nosdoden.cs:2145`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Kill the adds as they spawn (the route already puts script-spawned adds near the front first).
- **adds** (verified): Spawns bone commander (GhostSkeletalCommander): from SummonSkeletalCommander; count 1 spawn call in SummonSkeletalCommander `Nosdoden.cs:2231`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Kill the adds as they spawn (the route already puts script-spawned adds near the front first).
- **adds** (verified): Spawns bonemender (SkeletalCommanderHealer): from SummonBonemender; count 1 spawn call in SummonBonemender `Nosdoden.cs:2934`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Kill the adds as they spawn (the route already puts script-spawned adds near the front first).
- **phase** (verified): At 90/80/70/60/50/40/30/20/10% health: one essence wave each (SpawnEssences). `Nosdoden.cs:586`. *needs new raid AI*: Damage pacing: a raid that blows through two thresholds at once doubles the wave.
- **reset** (verified): Resets when its aggro list is empty: walks home at full health, adds cleared `Nosdoden.cs:541`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Keep someone on its aggro list until it dies; a wipe resets it (the 8 s quiet rule keeps a big raid's gaps from resetting it).
- **linked_pack** (verified): Brings others into the fight: every NPC with PackageID "NosdodenGhostSpiritmaster" (none in the world database) `Nosdoden.cs:1447`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Pull its linked pack with it on purpose: clear them first or fight them at the muster.
- **linked_pack** (verified): Brings others into the fight: every NPC with PackageID "NosdodenGhostBonedancer" (none in the world database) `Nosdoden.cs:1511`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Pull its linked pack with it on purpose: clear them first or fight them at the muster.
- **linked_pack** (verified): Brings others into the fight: every NPC with PackageID "NosdodenGhostHunter" (none in the world database) `Nosdoden.cs:1990`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Pull its linked pack with it on purpose: clear them first or fight them at the muster.
- **linked_pack** (verified): Brings others into the fight: every NPC with PackageID "NosdodenGhostSpiritmaster" (none in the world database) `Nosdoden.cs:2787`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Pull its linked pack with it on purpose: clear them first or fight them at the muster.
- **linked_pack** (verified): Brings others into the fight: every NPC with PackageID "NosdodenGhostBonedancer" (none in the world database) `Nosdoden.cs:2899`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Pull its linked pack with it on purpose: clear them first or fight them at the muster.
- **heal** (verified): Has a heal spell: Heal (Heal, value 400, target REALM) `Nosdoden.cs:2257`. *needs new raid AI*: Kill or interrupt whatever heals it first.
- **heal** (verified): Has a heal spell: Heal (Heal, value 250, target REALM) `Nosdoden.cs:3086`. *needs new raid AI*: Kill or interrupt whatever heals it first.

### 4. Xanxicar (Crystal Cave): stomps and champions

**Run evidence:** overnight notes, day run 2026-11-05: Defeated at Xanxicar with support 199 (required 150), Special Forces joined, one named kill.

**Why it blocks:** Four announced point-blank stomps on everyone around him plus an endless chain of level 65 epic champions from 50%; the throw and glare skip bots, so the danger is the stomp and the champions.

**Proposed tactic:** Casters and healers outside the stomp radius on each announcement; one party per champion, killed away from the tank; healers top up before 80/60/40/20%.

Rules of Xanxicar (`source/server/GameServer/scripts/namedmobs/AvalonCity/CrystalCave/Xanxicar.cs`):

- **adds** (verified): At 50%: one Xanxicarian Champion (GameEpicNPC, level 65); after it dies another follows every 15-25 s while he fights. `Xanxicar.cs:386`. *needs new raid AI*: One party holds and kills each Champion away from the main tank. The route treats an epic NPC as its own encounter, not his add.
- **adds** (verified): Spawns XanxicarianChampion: on a timer (Util.Random(15000, 25000) ms); count 1 spawn call in SpawnMoreAdds `Xanxicar.cs:400`. *needs new raid AI*: Its adds are epic NPCs: the route treats them as separate encounters, not adds. Assign parties to them as they spawn.
- **phase** (verified): At 80/60/40/20% health: announces and casts Xanxicar's Stomp, a point-blank AoE on everything around him (1 s after the announcement). `Xanxicar.cs:341`. *needs new raid AI*: On the announcement casters, healers and archers step out of the stomp radius; melee eat it with full health. Healers top up before each threshold.
- **phase** (verified): At 50% health (Think): starts timer SpawnMoreAdds; calls SpawnAdds `Xanxicar.cs:361`. *needs new raid AI*: Know the threshold: get ready for what it starts (adds, a big hit) before pushing it past.
- **reset** (verified): Resets when its aggro list is empty: walks home at full health, adds cleared `Xanxicar.cs:299`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Keep someone on its aggro list until it dies; a wipe resets it (the 8 s quiet rule keeps a big raid's gaps from resetting it).
- **teleport** (verified): ThrowPlayer: every 20-40 s a random raider within 2,500 (not his target; real players, and bots since 1.2) is moved to 32338,32387,16539 ('hurled into the air'). `Xanxicar.cs:224`. *easy for the current raid AI*: A thrown bot lands with the player's fall damage and walks back to its party, or recalls after 5 s with no way back (EncounterDisplacementRecovery); healers heal it first and its party calls it in /group.

### 5. Galladoria's last room: the four primals before Olcasgean

**Proposed tactic:** Assign a group per primal: ranged on Air, Earthmenders before Earth, stop on Water while it heals, keep out of fire trails. Then two raid halves for Olcasgean and his copy.

- **adds** (verified): Spawns FirePrimal: on a timer (via SpawnPrimals); count 1 spawn call in SpawnFire `OlcasgeanAndPrimals.cs:172`. *needs new raid AI*: Its adds are epic NPCs: the route treats them as separate encounters, not adds. Assign parties to them as they spawn.

### 6. Spindler Broodmother and Xaga's fairies (Galladoria)

**Run evidence:** overnight notes, build 8d7baa3 (2026-11-05): Hibernia epic wiped at Spindler Broodmother 15:08 and at Tine 15:37.

**Why it blocks:** Steady spindler adds and a 20-25-add burst on her death; Xaga, Tine and Beatha pull each other in.

**Proposed tactic:** Spindler: an add party on newborns, AoE on her corpse. Xaga: pull all three together on purpose and kill the fairies first.

Rules of Spindler Broodmother (`source/server/GameServer/scripts/namedmobs/Galladoria/SpindlerBroodmother.cs`):

- **reset** (verified): Resets when its aggro list is empty: walks home at full health, adds cleared (waits until 8 s out of combat: EpicEncounterMechanics.FightIsOver) `SpindlerBroodmother.cs:169`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Keep someone on its aggro list until it dies; a wipe resets it (the 8 s quiet rule keeps a big raid's gaps from resetting it).
- **teleport** (verified): Teleports TeleportTarget to a fixed spot (MoveTo Body.CurrentRegionID, 21115, 53483, 11286, 2100) `SpindlerBroodmother.cs:357`. *off today*: Off today (AllowDisplacement is false). To play it: recover the moved raider: walk back to the fight, or free them.
- **linked_pack** (verified): Brings others into the fight `SpindlerBroodmother.cs:482`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Pull its linked pack with it on purpose: clear them first or fight them at the muster.

### 7. Princes Ba'alorien and Asmoien (Darkness Falls, no raid AI)

**Run evidence:** overnight-2026-11-04 checks 2-3: Albion and Hibernia Darkness Falls raids each Defeated after two wipes at Prince Ba'alorien.

**Proposed tactic:** Pet classes park pets out of reach (more than 2,000) for the princes. Darkness Falls has no raid AI; this is a proposal only.

Never a bot: before 1.2 the check (a controlled brain) matched companion bots and gamebots too and ate one every 5 s (EpicEncounterMechanics.IsDevourablePet). `PrinceBaalorien.cs:109`. *needs new raid AI*: Pet classes leave or park their pets more than 2,000 away before the pull and fight without them.

### 9. Aidon the Archwizard (Hall of the Corrupt)

**Run evidence:** overnight-2026-11-04 check 5: Albion Hall of the Corrupt wiped at Aidon 03:25 (living 13 of 56) and 03:58, Defeated. Again 2026-11-05 (168f55d): 22:38 switch to Aidon without a muster, 31 engaged / 31 fallen at 99%, counted as a wipe with 56 living; the next muster on the ramp gathered 0-2 of 75. Later the same night (server.log 22:50-23:16) the regroup filled (FINAL_ENGAGE 22:58:13 75/75, then 23:00 and 23:02 106/53) but all three pulls walked to the front left from the 22:38 switch (31686,35683,14888) while he stood at home (31353,37634,14873); the closest raider ended 1,979 away and REALM_EXPEDITION_PULL_UNREACHED set him aside at 23:04. The front is now resolved again from his home (REALM_EXPEDITION_FRONT_REFRESHED).

**Why it blocks:** A permanent damage shield, a 500 Heat AoE every 10-15 s, and four illusions (5,000 health, 300 AoE each) that come back every 30-45 s once all are dead; tether 1,500.

- **reset** (verified): Resets when its aggro list is empty: walks home at full health, adds cleared `AidonTheArchwizard.cs:205`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Keep someone on its aggro list until it dies; a wipe resets it (the 8 s quiet rule keeps a big raid's gaps from resetting it).
- **reset** (verified): Resets while drawn past its tether: aggro cleared, back to full health `AidonTheArchwizard.cs:232`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Keep someone on its aggro list until it dies; a wipe resets it (the 8 s quiet rule keeps a big raid's gaps from resetting it).
- **reset** (verified): Resets after 30 s out of combat: aggro cleared, full health `AidonTheArchwizard.cs:235`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Keep someone on its aggro list until it dies; a wipe resets it (the 8 s quiet rule keeps a big raid's gaps from resetting it).
- **linked_pack** (verified): Brings others into the fight: AidonCopyAirBrain `AidonTheArchwizard.cs:184`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Pull its linked pack with it on purpose: clear them first or fight them at the muster.

### 10. Conservator and the Organic-Energy Mechanism (Galladoria)

**Run evidence:** Code history (RealmRaidDungeonRoute): run 25 Conservator wiped the raid twice (45%, 42%); 2026-10-20 the Organic-Energy Mechanism wiped 152 of 156 and the regroup did worse.

**Why it blocks:** Raw damage plus adds every 10 s (3-5 bottom feeders) and a damage shield on the Mechanism; Conservator's stand-near-the-spawn rule is off today.

**Proposed tactic:** Add party on the feeders; melee respect the Mechanism's shield; fight Conservator at his spawn.

Rules of Conservator (`source/server/GameServer/scripts/namedmobs/Galladoria/Conservator.cs`):

- **immune_while** (verified): Takes no damage unless the attacker stands near its spawn (if (!source.IsWithinRadius(spawn,800))). `Conservator.cs:35`. *off today*: Off today (AllowPositionPuzzle is false). To play it: attack only from inside the allowed area.
- **reset** (verified): Resets when its aggro list is empty: walks home at full health (waits until 8 s out of combat: EpicEncounterMechanics.FightIsOver) `Conservator.cs:202`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Keep someone on its aggro list until it dies; a wipe resets it (the 8 s quiet rule keeps a big raid's gaps from resetting it).
- **reset** (verified): Resets after 30 s out of combat: aggro cleared, full health `Conservator.cs:214`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Keep someone on its aggro list until it dies; a wipe resets it (the 8 s quiet rule keeps a big raid's gaps from resetting it).

Rules of Organic-Energy Mechanism (`source/server/GameServer/scripts/namedmobs/Galladoria/Organic-EnergyMechanism.cs`):

- **reflect** (verified): Has a damage shield / reflect spell: Shield of World Soul (DamageShield, damage 150, target SELF) `Organic-EnergyMechanism.cs:307`. *needs new raid AI*: Check who it punishes; switch damage type or attacker if it reflects.
- **adds** (verified): Spawns Summoned Bottom Feeder (OEMAdd): on a timer (10000 ms); count about 3-5 (loop to a random bound) `Organic-EnergyMechanism.cs:247`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Kill the adds as they spawn (the route already puts script-spawned adds near the front first).
- **reset** (verified): Resets after 40 s out of combat: aggro cleared, full health `Organic-EnergyMechanism.cs:198`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Keep someone on its aggro list until it dies; a wipe resets it (the 8 s quiet rule keeps a big raid's gaps from resetting it).
- **reset** (verified): Resets when its aggro list is empty: walks home at full health, adds cleared (waits until 8 s out of combat: EpicEncounterMechanics.FightIsOver) `Organic-EnergyMechanism.cs:199`. *easy for the current raid AI*: Keep someone on its aggro list until it dies; a wipe resets it (the 8 s quiet rule keeps a big raid's gaps from resetting it).

## Open questions for the owner

- Upstream helper packs: add the 38 spawns our world lacks (AncientOakBaf 14, CaimheulBaf 5, CailleachUragaigBaf 3, RevenantBaf 11, RunilBaf 4) as a world update, or leave those packs empty?
- Scurceol's orbs are level 90 upstream too. Keep them, or lower them only when bots must kill them?
- Which restored mechanic should the raid AI learn first? Each is off until its tactic exists; the catalog lists them under 'Restored behind switches'.
- Answered by the owner (2026-11-06): throws and other unique triggers target bots too. Since 1.2 the boss scripts pick through EncounterTargets (real players and bots); picks that move their victim include bots once EncounterTargets.BotsCanBeDisplaced is on, and the mechanics still listed under 'Player-only mechanics' are those moves plus the ones left player-only on purpose.

#### Apocalypse (`Apocalypse`)

`source/server/GameServer/scripts/namedmobs/CaerSidi/ApocalypseAnd4Horsemans .cs` lines 2132-2270. level 87; 300,000 health; npc template 60157955; raids: epic-albion (final); raid AI: generic dungeon route; spawned by Funus.

1.2 (wave 2): the first two Harbingers of Fate come once after his fourth raider kill (FirstHarbingersDue: kills >= 4; the original's '== 4' on a 2-second check missed it when two died at once, and repeated it while the count stayed at 4); a harbinger's death now lowers the live count, so the 50% pair (spawned only while none lives) can come after the first pair is dead. Up to four harbingers in a fight instead of two. 1.2 (wave 3): the room barrier (ApocInitializator.DontAllowLeaveRoom: anyone within 150 of the door 29,459,26,401 while a horseman or Apocalypse fights is moved to the middle 29,469,25,244) is on for players and bots (AllowDisplacement and AllowBotDisplacement for ApocInitializator). The raid's muster and every tactic stand-off for the room's encounters are kept within 800 of the middle, the route doesn't leave the room mid-fight, and a bot moved back stays inside (EpicEncounterTactics.HorsemenRoom); a repeated move is logged (EPIC_DISPLACEMENT_BOUNCED). The room's shape was never checked against the navmesh.

`source/server/GameServer/scripts/namedmobs/CaerSidi/LordSanguis.cs` lines 225-302. level 81; 100,000 health; npc template 60163267; raids: epic-albion (named); raid AI: generic dungeon route; spawned by LordSanguis.

At 5% health he turns untargetable (CANTTARGET) until the fight resets; area spells and running damage over time still hit him. 1.2: bots never fight an untargetable NPC, so the raid would stall on his last 5%; now offensive casters keep him as their target and finish him with spells (EpicEncounterTactics.FinishedBySpells), and the dungeon route keeps him as a target. ### Cuuldurach's lair (Sheeroe Hills) (region 200)

#### Cuuldurach the Glimmer King (`HibCuuldurach`)

`source/server/GameServer/scripts/namedmobs/Dragons/HibCuuldurach.cs` lines 13-268. level 80; 300,000 health; npc template 678903; raids: dragon-hibernia (final); raid AI: dragon fight.

Dragons have their own raid fight (RealmRaidDragonFight). Glare and the roaming glare pick from the whole raid, bots included (EncounterTargets, 1.2); before 1.2 they picked real players only. 1.2: the wing-gust throw is back (OpenDAoC-Core 3513bad, DragonEncounterThrow) and on for this dragon (AllowDragonThrow and AllowBotDisplacement per encounter): bots are thrown too and recover (EncounterDisplacementRecovery). The messenger waves are back behind AllowDragonMessengers, on for this dragon since 1.2 wave 2: the raid's casters and archers kill the runners. Other adds come from DragonEncounterAdds: at most six in a fight, one every 20 s at most, at 85/70/55/40/25/10% health.

#### Olcasgean Initializator (`OlcasgeanInitializator`)

`source/server/GameServer/scripts/namedmobs/Galladoria/OlcasgeanAndPrimals.cs` lines 17-49. level 50; 10,000 health; raids: epic-hibernia (monster on the way (not an epic NPC)); raid AI: generic dungeon route.

Not a monster but the event controller for Galladoria's last room. A raid bot within 1,500 of the waterfall bridge (39652,60831,11893) starts it (a real player must stand within 350); 20 s of messages later it spawns the four Corrupt Primals, the four Earthmenders and eleven Watery Vortices. Olcasgean wakes 25 s after all four primals are dead (OIBrain.DeadPrimalsCount >= 4). 1.2 wave 2: AllowGalladoriaBridgeSpot is on: a raider must stand within 350 of the spot (the route stages its first party there); a raid bot that has stood within 1,500 for 3 minutes still starts it.

#### Olcasgean (`Olcasgean`)

Level 87, 250,000 health, the final boss. Asleep until all four primals are dead. Spawns a copy of himself (Olcasgean2, a second 250,000-health body) when he wakes. ### Gjalpinulva's lair (Malmohus) (region 100)

#### Gjalpinulva (`MidGjalpinulva`)

`source/server/GameServer/scripts/namedmobs/Dragons/MidGjalpinulva.cs` lines 13-266. level 80; 300,000 health; npc template 694189; raids: dragon-midgard (final); raid AI: dragon fight.

Dragons have their own raid fight (RealmRaidDragonFight). Glare and the roaming glare pick from the whole raid, bots included (EncounterTargets, 1.2); before 1.2 they picked real players only. 1.2: the wing-gust throw is back (OpenDAoC-Core 3513bad, DragonEncounterThrow) and on for this dragon (AllowDragonThrow and AllowBotDisplacement per encounter): bots are thrown too and recover (EncounterDisplacementRecovery). The messenger waves are back behind AllowDragonMessengers, on for this dragon since 1.2 wave 2: the raid's casters and archers kill the runners. Other adds come from DragonEncounterAdds: at most six in a fight, one every 20 s at most, at 85/70/55/40/25/10% health.

### Golestandt's lair (Dartmoor) (region 1)

#### Golestandt (`AlbGolestandt`)

`source/server/GameServer/scripts/namedmobs/Dragons/AlbGolestandt.cs` lines 13-267. level 80; 300,000 health; npc template 60157497; raids: dragon-albion (final); raid AI: dragon fight.

Dragons have their own raid fight (RealmRaidDragonFight). Glare and the roaming glare pick from the whole raid, bots included (EncounterTargets, 1.2); before 1.2 they picked real players only. 1.2: the wing-gust throw is back (OpenDAoC-Core 3513bad, DragonEncounterThrow) and on for this dragon (AllowDragonThrow and AllowBotDisplacement per encounter): bots are thrown too and recover (EncounterDisplacementRecovery). The messenger waves are back behind AllowDragonMessengers, on for this dragon since 1.2 wave 2: the raid's casters and archers kill the runners. Other adds come from DragonEncounterAdds: at most six in a fight, one every 20 s at most, at 85/70/55/40/25/10% health.

### Hall of the Corrupt (region 277)

#### Aidon the Archwizard (`AidonTheArchwizard`)

`source/server/GameServer/scripts/namedmobs/HallOfTheCorrupt/AidonTheArchwizard.cs` lines 10-143. level 75; 40,000 health; npc template 7721; raids: dungeon-albion-corrupt (final); raid AI: generic dungeon route + tactics (EpicEncounterTactics).

Level 75, 40,000 health, 30/40% resists, tether 1,500 (immune past it). What stops damage: only the tether; his permanent Damage Shield (Heat, 100 per hit back on melee attackers, recast whenever it is not on him) does not reduce damage taken, so he is killable by design; no damage type bypasses anything and no dispel is needed (he recasts it at once). What kills raids: his four illusions (5,000 health each, spawned at fixed spots the first time he is attacked) each cast a 300 area hit in 350 around their target every brain turn, and he casts Aidon's Fire (500 Heat, radius 350) every 10-15 s. A new set of illusions comes 30-45 s after all four are dead, never while one lives. No player-only mechanic is needed to beat him, so no switch has to flip.

| Kind | Rule | Where | Confidence | Switch | Difficulty | Proposed tactic |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| immune_while / out_of_tether | Takes no damage while it is drawn past its tether. | `AidonTheArchwizard.cs:41` | read, verified |  | easy for the current raid AI | Fight inside the tether: stop hitting it once it is drawn out and lead it home (AutonomousTetherReset already does this). |
| reflect | Has a damage shield / reflect spell: Aidon's Damage Shield (DamageShield, damage 100, target SELF) | `AidonTheArchwizard.cs:398` | read, verified |  | needs new raid AI | Check who it punishes; switch damage type or attacker if it reflects. |
| adds | Spawns 4 kinds of add (Illusion of Aidon the Archwizard (AidonCopyAir), Illusion of Aidon the Archwizard (AidonCopyFire), Illusion of Aidon the Archwizard (AidonCopyEarth), Illusion of Aidon the Archwizard (AidonCopyIce)): on a timer (Util.Random(30000, 45000) ms) | `AidonTheArchwizard.cs:279` | inferred, verified |  | easy for the current raid AI | Kill the adds as they spawn (the route already puts script-spawned adds near the front first). |

#### Orshom Brong (`Orshom`)

`source/server/GameServer/scripts/namedmobs/Krondon/OrylleAndOrshom.cs` lines 182-254. level 80; 30,000 health; raids: dungeon-albion-krondon (named); raid AI: generic dungeon route + tactics (EpicEncounterTactics).

Level 80, 30,000 health, 20% melee and 30% magic resists, tether 3,800. When his fight starts he lights Orshom's Fire (OrshomFire) at 31406,69599,15605, about 900 from his spawn: a GameEpicNPC that never moves or melees (MaxSpeedBase 0, empty StartAttack), takes damage only of type Cold, has 10,000 health and RespawnInterval -1, aggro range 2,500, and casts Fire Burn on its own spot every 3 s (500 Heat, radius 300). His reset removes it. Orylle throws a random real player within 2,500 into it every 15-20 s while it burns (GetPlayersInRadius: never a bot). At 50% Orshom pulls Orylle (level 83, 40,000 health) into the fight; Orylle pulls him too when she is attacked.

#### Scurceol Hyrde (`ScurceolHyrde`)

`source/server/GameServer/scripts/namedmobs/Krondon/ScurceolHyrde.cs` lines 10-155. level 81; 30,000 health; raids: dungeon-albion-krondon (named); raid AI: generic dungeon route + tactics (EpicEncounterTactics).

Immune while any of his four Lyft Miht orbs lives. The orbs (level 90, 15,000 health, 40% melee and 50% magic resists, AF 250) float 450-790 above the room floor at its four corners (51236,20849 / 51316,19228 / 52702,19214 / 52713,20841; floor about 16964), never attack (StartAttack is empty), have a 250 aggro range and RespawnInterval -1. Once dead they stay dead: his reset does not bring them back, only his own respawn (SpawnOrbs runs in AddToWorld). He gains 60 Strength while any orb lives.

Bots are thrown too (AllowBotDisplacement on); the camp's own monsters attack, never bots or Moran.

Flood guard (EncounterFloodGuard 'OFGreenKnight.Trees'): at most 70 trees a wave, the period size of the Albion dragon raid whose way he is on. 1.2 (wave 3): his fight starts by a real player's whisper ("defend it") as before, or for a bot raid: eight or more level 50 raiders of an autonomous raid expedition standing within 1,000 of him for 20 s, and the one nearest him challenges him (OFGreenKnight.TryStartFight). ### Summoner's Hall (region 248)

#### Grand Summoner Govannon (`GrandSummonerGovannon`)

`source/server/GameServer/scripts/namedmobs/SummonersHall/GrandSummonerGovannon.cs` lines 11-208. level 80; npc template 18801; raids: summoners-albion (final), summoners-midgard (final), summoners-hibernia (final); raid AI: generic dungeon route + tactics (EpicEncounterTactics).

Level 80, 200,000 health (AllowGovannonFullHealth, off by default until a played check, server property epic_govannon_full_health; on, the original 300,000), 40% melee and 70% magic resists, tether 1,300. At 80% he gains strength (650) and summons a sacrifice and a demon (6,000 health each, they never attack) at 31018,40889 and 33215,40883; they walk at 35 to his altar (32063,40896, beside his spawn) and die there, and only if both reach it the Shade of Aelfgar comes (10,000 health, a 500-radius stun, sends a raider to the gallery, bots too since 1.2). Killed on the way, no Shade. At 50% he turns into a demon (model 636): healed to full, strength 800, a bleed on melee hits, and every 20-35 s Govannon's Shroud of Agony (Matter, 120 every 2 s for 24 s, radius 300 around his target); the sacrifice and demon come again at 80% of the demon form.

#### Nosdoden (`Nosdoden`)

`source/server/GameServer/scripts/namedmobs/Trollheim/Nosdoden.cs` lines 14-365. level 80; 100,000 health; npc template 60164545; raids: dungeon-midgard-trollheim (final); raid AI: generic dungeon route + tactics (EpicEncounterTactics).

Level 80, 100,000 health. Every 10% from 90% down to 10% he spawns a wave of about 12-18 'essence of fallen' (level 60-62, 2,500 health, aggro 1,500) at his feet: nine waves, roughly 110-160 adds in one fight. His Acidic cloud (Body DoT 150 for 60 s, radius 500) and Call of Void (Cold 1,100, radius 550) go on a random raider within 2,000 (real players and bots since 1.2, EncounterTargets). Spirits of the fallen are made from dead raiders, bots included (since 1.2). A bot raid larger than Trollheim's period raid (64) gets at most 64 live spirits.
